Step 6: Generating first solution code: 
Generated Response: ```json
{
  "solution_code": {
    "sample_input": "4\n2 50\n950 95\n13 37\n3 10",
    "sample_output": "Case #1: 20.710678118654748\nCase #2: 0.005129467915043762\nCase #3: 2.940819927087601\nCase #4: 11.544346900318839",
    "language": "Python",
    "code": "def calculate_increase(N, P):\n    if P >= 99:\n        return 0.0\n    target_prob = (1 - (1 - P / 100) ** N) ** (1 / (N - 1))\n    low, high = 0, 100\n    while high - low > 1e-6:\n        mid = (low + high) / 2\n        if (1 - (1 - mid / 100) ** (N - 1)) >= target_prob:\n            high = mid\n        else:\n            low = mid\n    return high - P\n\ndef main():\n    import sys\n    input = sys.stdin.read\n    data = input().split()\n    index = 0\n    T = int(data[index])\n    index += 1\n    results = []\n    for _ in range(T):\n        N = int(data[index])\n        index += 1\n        P = float(data[index])\n        index += 1\n        increase = calculate_increase(N, P)\n        results.append(f'Case #{_ + 1}: {increase:.10f}')\n    print('\\n'.join(results))\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()"
  }
}
```

```json
    "code": "import heapq\n\ndef find_closest_stone_to_goal(test_cases):\n    results = []\n    for i, (N, G, energies) in enumerate(test_cases, start=1):\n        stones = [(e, 0) for e in energies]\n        heapq.heapify(stones)\n        while len(stones) > 1:\n            e1, p1 = heapq.heappop(stones)\n            e2, p2 = stones[0]\n            if p1 + e1 >= G or p2 + e2 >= G:\n                break\n            if e1 >= e2:\n                heapq.heapreplace(stones, (-e1 - e2, p1 + e1))\n            else:\n                heapq.heapreplace(stones, (-e2, p2 + e2))\n        closest_stone = min(stones, key=lambda x: (abs(x[1] + x[0] - G), x[1]))\n        results.append(f'Case #{i}: {stones.index(closest_stone) + 1} {abs(closest_stone[1] + closest_stone[0] - G)}')\n    return results\n\ndef main():\n    input()\n    test_cases = []\n    for _ in range(int(input())):\n        N, G = map(int, input().split())\n        energies = [int(input()) for _ in range(N)]\n        test_cases.append((N, G, energies))\n    results = find_closest_stone_to_goal(test_cases)\n    for result in results:\n        print(result)\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()"
  }
}
```

**Updated Code Snippet**:
```python
def find_closest_stone_to_goal(T):
    results = []
    for t in range(1, T + 1):
        line = input().split()
        if len(line) < 2:
            results.append(f"Invalid input for Case #{t}")
            continue
        N = int(line[0])
        G = int(line[1])
        stones = [(int(line[i]), i - 1) for i in range(2, len(line))]
        heapq.heapify(stones)
        positions = [0] * N
        while stones:
            energy, idx = heapq.heappop(stones)
            if positions[idx] >= G:
                break
            positions[idx] += energy
            if positions[idx] >= G:
                break
            for j in range(idx + 1, N):
                if positions[j] == positions[idx]:
                    stones[j] = (stones[j][0] + energy, j)
                    heapq.heapify(stones)
                    break
        closest_distance = float('inf')
        closest_idx = -1
        for i in range(N):
            if positions[i] < G and abs(positions[i] - G) < closest_distance:
                closest_distance = abs(positions[i] - G)
                closest_idx = i
        results.append(f"Case #{t}: {closest_idx + 1} {closest_distance}")
    return '\n'.join(results)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    T = int(input())
    print(find_closest_stone_to_goal(T))
```

```json
    "code": "import heapq\n\ndef find_closest_stone_to_goal():\n    T = int(input())\n    results = []\n    for _ in range(T):\n        N, G = map(int, input().split())\n        stones = [-int(input()) for _ in range(N)]\n        heapq.heapify(stones)\n        while stones:\n            energy = -heapq.heappop(stones)\n            if energy >= G:\n                results.append((1, energy - G))\n                break\n            energy -= 1\n            if stones:\n                heapq.heappush(stones, -(stones[0] + energy))\n        else:\n            results.append((None, None))\n    for i, (index, distance) in enumerate(results, start=1):\n        if index is None:\n            print(f\"Case #{i}: No stone can reach the goal\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"Case #{i}: {index} {distance}\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    find_closest_stone_to_goal()"
```

Step 6: Generating first solution code: 
Generated Response: ```json
{
  "solution_code": {
    "sample_input": "2\n5\n7\n2",
    "sample_output": "Case #1: 1 2",
    "language": "Python",
    "code": "import heapq\n\ndef simulate_stone_movement(n, g, energies):\n    stones = [(e, i) for i, e in enumerate(energies)]\n    heapq.heapify(stones)\n    positions = [0] * n\n    distances = [float('inf')] * n\n    moves = [0] * n\n\n    while stones:\n        e, i = heapq.heappop(stones)\n        if positions[i] + e >= g:\n            positions[i] += e - (positions[i] + e - g)\n            break\n        positions[i] += e\n        moves[i] += e\n        if positions[i] + 1 < n and moves[positions[i] + 1] > moves[i]:\n            heapq.heappush(stones, (moves[positions[i] + 1] - moves[i], positions[i] + 1))\n            moves[positions[i] + 1] -= moves[i]\n        elif positions[i] + 1 < n and moves[positions[i] + 1] == moves[i]:\n            heapq.heappush(stones, (moves[positions[i] + 1] - moves[i], positions[i] + 1))\n            moves[positions[i] + 1] -= moves[i]\n\n    closest_distance = float('inf')\n    closest_index = -1\n    for i in range(n):\n        dist = abs(positions[i] - g)\n        if dist < closest_distance or (dist == closest_distance and i < closest_index):\n            closest_distance = dist\n            closest_index = i\n\n    return f'Case #{closest_index + 1}: {closest_index} {closest_distance}'\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    import sys\n    input = sys.stdin.read\n    data = input().split()\n    t = int(data[0])\n    index = 1\n    results = []\n    for _ in range(t):\n        n = int(data[index])\n        g = int(data[index + 1])\n        energies = [int(data[index + 2 + i]) for i in range(n)]\n        index += 2 + n\n        results.append(simulate_stone_movement(n, g, energies))\n    print('\\n'.join(results))"
  }
}
```

### Proposed Code:
```python
def can_cross_bridge(N, K, times):
    times.sort()
    if max(times) > K:
        return 'NO'
    
    left = 0
    right = N - 1
    while left < right:
        if times[left] + times[right] > K:
            return 'NO'
        left += 1
        right -= 1
    
    return 'YES'

def main():
    import sys
    input = sys.stdin.read
    data = input().split()
    index = 0
    T = int(data[index])
    index += 1
    results = []
    for _ in range(T):
        N = int(data[index])
        index += 1
        K = int(data[index])
        index += 1
        times = [int(data[index]) for _ in range(N)]
        index += N
        result = can_cross_bridge(N, K, times)
        results.append(result)
    for i, result in enumerate(results, start=1):
        print(f'Case #{i}: {result}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### Explanation:
1. **Optimized Algorithm**: The `can_cross_bridge` function now uses a greedy approach. It sorts the list of crossing times and tries to pair the fastest and slowest travelers. If the sum of their crossing times exceeds `K`, it returns 'NO'. Otherwise, it continues until all travelers are paired.
2. **Efficiency**: This approach significantly reduces the number of operations compared to the original implementation, making it suitable for handling larger inputs efficiently.
3. **Safety Checks**: The function already includes checks to ensure that the maximum crossing time is within the allowed limit. Additional safety checks can be added if needed, such as validating the input values.

```json
    "code": "import heapq\n\ndef simulate_stone_collision(n, g, energies):\n    stones = [(e, 0) for e in energies]\n    heapq.heapify(stones)\n    while len(stones) > 1:\n        e1, p1 = heapq.heappop(stones)\n        e2, p2 = heapq.heappop(stones)\n        if p1 + e1 <= g:\n            heapq.heappush(stones, (e1 + e2, p1 + e1))\n        else:\n            heapq.heappush(stones, (e1 - (g - p1), g))\n            heapq.heappush(stones, (e2, p2))\n    return stones[0][1], abs(g - stones[0][1])\n\ndef main():\n    t = int(input())\n    results = []\n    for i in range(t):\n        n, g = map(int, input().split())\n        energies = [int(input()) for _ in range(n)]\n        result = simulate_stone_collision(n, g, energies)\n        results.append(f'Case #{i+1}: {result[0] + 1} {result[1]}\n')\n    print(''.join(results))\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()"
```

```json
    "language": "Python",
```

```json
    "language": "Python",
```
```json
    "code": "def simulate_stone_movement(n, g, energies):\n    positions = [0] * n\n    while True:\n        moved = False\n        for i in range(n - 1):\n            if positions[i] + energies[i] > positions[i + 1]:\n                positions[i + 1] += energies[i]\n                energies[i] = 0\n                moved = True\n            elif positions[i] + energies[i] <= positions[i + 1]:\n                energies[i] -= (positions[i + 1] - positions[i])\n                positions[i] = positions[i + 1]\n        if not moved:\n            break\n    return min(range(n), key=lambda x: abs(positions[x] - g)), abs(positions[min(range(n), key=lambda x: abs(positions[x] - g))) - g)\n\ndef main():\n    import sys\n    input = sys.stdin.read\n    data = input().split()\n    index = 0\n    t = int(data[index])\n    index += 1\n    results = []\n    for _ in range(t):\n        n = int(data[index])\n        index += 1\n        g = int(data[index])\n        index += 1\n        energies = list(map(int, data[index:index + n]))\n        index += n\n        result = simulate_stone_movement(n, g, energies)\n        results.append(f'Case #{len(results) + 1}: {result[0] + 1} {result[1]}\n')\n    print(''.join(results))\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()"
```
